Source file(s):
  documentations_phase_2\approved_sample_tasks\d977bddfb3d94934b3c906b1fe61bae8-SWARMBENCH-HIERARCHICAL-PLANNING-OPERATIONS-US-POWERGRID-WMP-DECISION-SYNTHESIS\decomposition.yaml
```yaml
coordination_pattern: hierarchical

# Genuine 3-tier hierarchy (dag_depth = 3, widest tier = 18):
#
#   Tier 1  - 18 Decision readers (one per Decision: a draft and a final for each of
#             the 9 utilities). Each reader discovers ITS Decision from the Energy
#             Safety 2025 WMP index page, fetches the e-filing PDF, reads it, and
#             writes grounded findings to /logs/agent/findings_<utility>_<version>.csv.
#   Tier 2  - 3 regional consolidators. Each consumes the SIX reader shards in its
#             region (3 utilities x draft+final), de-duplicates them, notes what
#             changed between each utility's draft and final Decision, and writes a
#             regional consolidated file + theme notes. This tier consumes Tier-1
#             output before anything reaches the synthesizer, which is what makes the
#             depth real rather than a flat fan-out.
#   Tier 3  - 1 final-assembler. Consumes the three regional consolidations (and the
#             raw shards as backup) and produces the four deliverables.
#
# Index page (same for every reader):
# https://energysafety.ca.gov/what-we-do/electrical-infrastructure-safety/wildfire-mitigation-and-safety/wildfire-mitigation-plans/2025-wildfire-mitigation-plans/
#
# Every reader writes its findings DIRECTLY to /logs/agent/ (never /workspace) so a
# partial run still leaves a scorable ledger; the final-assembler merges from the raw
# shards, so a missing consolidator never loses the underlying findings.

sub_tasks:

  # ---------------- Tier 1: 18 Decision readers ----------------
  # Each reader: from the 2025 WMP index page, find its utility's 2025 WMP Update
  # Decision of the given version (the "Energy Safety Decision for ..." for final, the
  # "... Draft Decision ..." for draft) - NOT the change-order decisions, base-plan
  # revisions, errata or rejection letters. Follow the link to the e-filing PDF, read
  # it, and append grounded findings to its own shard with the exact ledger columns
  # (finding_id,utility,doc_id,topic,regulator_finding,quote,source_url). doc_id = the
  # e-filing file id; quote = exact verbatim text from the document; regulator_finding =
  # your own paraphrase, different text from the quote; never invent a finding.

  - id: reader-pge-draft
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the PG&E 2025 WMP Update DRAFT Decision. Discover it from the index page,
      fetch the e-filing PDF, read it, and write grounded findings (verbatim quotes,
      one topic each) to /logs/agent/findings_pge_draft.csv in the exact ledger schema.

  - id: reader-pge-final
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the PG&E 2025 WMP Update FINAL Decision. Discover it from the index page,
      fetch the e-filing PDF, read it, and write grounded findings to
      /logs/agent/findings_pge_final.csv in the exact ledger schema.

  - id: reader-sce-draft
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the Southern California Edison (SCE) 2025 WMP Update DRAFT Decision; discover
      from the index, fetch, read, and write findings to /logs/agent/findings_sce_draft.csv.

  - id: reader-sce-final
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the SCE 2025 WMP Update FINAL Decision; discover, fetch, read, and write
      findings to /logs/agent/findings_sce_final.csv.

  - id: reader-sdge-draft
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the San Diego Gas & Electric (SDG&E) 2025 WMP Update DRAFT Decision; discover,
      fetch, read, and write findings to /logs/agent/findings_sdge_draft.csv.

  - id: reader-sdge-final
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the SDG&E 2025 WMP Update FINAL Decision; discover, fetch, read, and write
      findings to /logs/agent/findings_sdge_final.csv.

  - id: reader-bves-draft
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the Bear Valley Electric Service (BVES) 2025 WMP Update DRAFT Decision;
      discover, fetch, read, and write findings to /logs/agent/findings_bves_draft.csv.

  - id: reader-bves-final
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the BVES 2025 WMP Update FINAL Decision; discover, fetch, read, and write
      findings to /logs/agent/findings_bves_final.csv.

  - id: reader-liberty-draft
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the Liberty Utilities 2025 WMP Update DRAFT Decision (not the change-order
      decision or rejection letter); discover, fetch, read, and write findings to
      /logs/agent/findings_liberty_draft.csv.

  - id: reader-liberty-final
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the Liberty Utilities 2025 WMP Update FINAL Decision; discover, fetch, read,
      and write findings to /logs/agent/findings_liberty_final.csv.

  - id: reader-pacificorp-draft
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the PacifiCorp 2025 WMP Update DRAFT Decision (not the errata or rejection
      letter); discover, fetch, read, and write findings to
      /logs/agent/findings_pacificorp_draft.csv.

  - id: reader-pacificorp-final
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the PacifiCorp 2025 WMP Update FINAL Decision; discover, fetch, read, and
      write findings to /logs/agent/findings_pacificorp_final.csv.

  - id: reader-hwt-draft
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the Horizon West Transmission (HWT) 2025 WMP Update DRAFT Decision; discover,
      fetch, read, and write findings to /logs/agent/findings_hwt_draft.csv.

  - id: reader-hwt-final
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the HWT 2025 WMP Update FINAL Decision; discover, fetch, read, and write
      findings to /logs/agent/findings_hwt_final.csv.

  - id: reader-tbc-draft
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the Trans Bay Cable (TBC) 2025 WMP Update DRAFT Decision; discover, fetch,
      read, and write findings to /logs/agent/findings_tbc_draft.csv.

  - id: reader-tbc-final
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the TBC 2025 WMP Update FINAL Decision; discover, fetch, read, and write
      findings to /logs/agent/findings_tbc_final.csv.

  - id: reader-lspower-draft
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the LS Power Grid California 2025 WMP Update DRAFT Decision (not the rejection
      letter); discover, fetch, read, and write findings to
      /logs/agent/findings_lspower_draft.csv.

  - id: reader-lspower-final
    parallel_group: read
    depends_on: []
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Read the LS Power Grid California 2025 WMP Update FINAL Decision; discover, fetch,
      read, and write findings to /logs/agent/findings_lspower_final.csv.

  # ---------------- Tier 2: 3 regional consolidators ----------------

  - id: consolidator-region-a
    parallel_group: consolidate
    depends_on: [reader-pge-draft, reader-pge-final, reader-sce-draft, reader-sce-final, reader-sdge-draft, reader-sdge-final]
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Consolidate the large-IOU region: PG&E, SCE and SDG&E. Read the six reader shards
      for these three utilities (/logs/agent/findings_{pge,sce,sdge}_{draft,final}.csv),
      de-duplicate findings, and for each utility note what the FINAL Decision changed
      relative to its DRAFT (new directives, dropped items, tightened language). Write a
      regional consolidated file /logs/agent/region_a_consolidated.csv (same ledger
      columns) plus a short /logs/agent/region_a_themes.md capturing the recurring
      directives and any outliers across these three utilities. Leave the reader shards
      in place.

  - id: consolidator-region-b
    parallel_group: consolidate
    depends_on: [reader-bves-draft, reader-bves-final, reader-liberty-draft, reader-liberty-final, reader-pacificorp-draft, reader-pacificorp-final]
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Consolidate the mid-size region: BVES, Liberty and PacifiCorp. Read the six reader
      shards for these utilities, de-duplicate, note each utility's draft-to-final
      changes, and write /logs/agent/region_b_consolidated.csv plus
      /logs/agent/region_b_themes.md (recurring directives and outliers across the three).
      Leave the reader shards in place.

  - id: consolidator-region-c
    parallel_group: consolidate
    depends_on: [reader-hwt-draft, reader-hwt-final, reader-tbc-draft, reader-tbc-final, reader-lspower-draft, reader-lspower-final]
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Consolidate the transmission-only region: HWT, TBC and LS Power Grid California.
      Read the six reader shards, de-duplicate, note each utility's draft-to-final
      changes, and write /logs/agent/region_c_consolidated.csv plus
      /logs/agent/region_c_themes.md. Leave the reader shards in place.

  # ---------------- Tier 3: final assembler ----------------

  - id: final-assembler
    parallel_group: assemble
    depends_on: [consolidator-region-a, consolidator-region-b, consolidator-region-c]
    subagent_type: explore
    description: >-
      Produce the four final deliverables under /logs/agent/ from the three regional
      consolidations: the merged findings ledger cross_utility_findings.csv (schema
      finding_id,utility,doc_id,topic,regulator_finding,quote,source_url); the written
      briefing reliability_briefing.docx; the three charts chart_findings_by_topic.png,
      chart_findings_by_utility.png and chart_directive_themes.png; and the deck
      briefing_deck.pptx.
```

--- REFERENCE 15 ---
Source file(s):
  documentations_phase_2\approved_sample_tasks\dcad144c9bc64475bbadfd61cc9b53b0-SWARMBENCH-HIERARCHICAL-PLANNING-OPERATIONS-CLINICAL-TRIAL-RISK-BASED-MONITORING\decomposition.yaml
```yaml
version: "1.0"
coordination_pattern: "hierarchical"
orchestrator_directive: >-
  Do NOT read, sort, count or process the trial records yourself, and do not write
  any deliverable yourself - your only job is to dispatch the sub-agents below as
  `task` calls (one call per sub_task) and pass each one the exact text of its
  description, then collect results. Each extraction sub_task already lists the exact
  NCT ids it owns, so you never have to partition anything - just copy the list into
  the task prompt.   Dispatch order, three levels: (1) dispatch the thirteen extraction-batch leaf
  workers and the five research leaf workers, in small batches of a few task calls at
  a time, not all at once; (2) once the thirteen extraction batches have returned
  dispatch the extraction-coordinator, and once the five research leaves have returned
  dispatch the research-coordinator - each coordinator consolidates its own group's
  outputs into a single master file; (3) once BOTH coordinators have returned,
  dispatch the two final assemblers together in the same message - the
  workbook-builder and the plan-writer - each of which reads the two master files and
  writes its own finished deliverable straight to /logs/agent. CRITICAL: every message
  you send while work remains MUST contain an actual `task` tool call. Never send a
  message that only says you are "about to" or "now dispatching" a task - a message
  with no `task` call ends the run immediately and fails it. When the coordinators
  finish, put the workbook-builder and plan-writer `task` calls in the SAME message;
  do not announce them and stop. The whole run FAILS unless BOTH
  /logs/agent/output.xlsx and /logs/agent/monitoring_plan.md exist when you finish.

sub_tasks:

    parallel_group: aggregate

      - africa-manager
      - asia-manager

      data.csv with one row per country (keep the exact order from the instruction) and

```yaml
coordination_pattern: pipeline

sub_tasks:

  - id: discover-repos
    parallel_group: discover
    depends_on: []
    description: >-
      Browse https://github.com/orgs/canada-ca/repositories?type=public and
      paginate through all pages. Collect the name and URL of every public
      repository. Write the full numbered list to /workspace/all_repos.json as
      an array of objects with index, name, and url fields. Do not spawn
      sub-agents. Do not skip any repositories.

  - id: manager-group-a
    parallel_group: audit
    depends_on: [discover-repos]
    description: >-
      You are a group manager. Read /workspace/all_repos.json and take entries
      index 1 to 10. Spawn exactly two workers simultaneously using the task tool
      and wait for both before reporting back. Do not audit repositories yourself.

      Worker 1 is a programmatic extractor. Give it repos 1-10 and instruct it
      to visit each repository main page and extract without reading file contents:
      primary_language, readme_exists, contributing_guide, last_commit_date,
      open_issues_count, maintained, and licence. It must write results to
      /workspace/prog_group_a.json keyed by repo name. It must not use the task tool.

sub_tasks:
- id: discovery--triage-router
  depends_on: []
  parallel_group: discovery
  agent_type: general
  description: "You are the Discovery & Triage Router (a manager). First run `mkdir -p /workspace/records`. Fetch the cohort manifest from https://github.com/ruturajs-turing/cofc-bid-protest-corpus/releases/download/v1/manifest.json -- it lists 48 opinions, each with protest_id, opinion_url (CourtListener), and text_url (pinned plain text). For EACH opinion, download it to disk with bash (`curl -sL \"$TEXT_URL\" -o /workspace/records/<protest_id>.txt`) and SKIM it (the caption plus the holding/ordering paragraphs near the end are enough) to assign a provisional primary protest ground -- one of jurisdiction-challenge (standing/jurisdiction/timeliness dispositive), evaluation-error (challenge to the agency's scoring/evaluation), process-defect (defective solicitation, unequal treatment, OCI, corrective-action disputes), or other. Do not read full texts into your context; rely on the saved files. Write a routing manifest to /workspace/routing.md listing, under four headings (one per ground), every protest_id assigned to that ground with its opinion_url and text_url. Return the routing manifest in your reply so each domain manager can pull its bucket."

- id: lead--jurisdiction-challenge
  depends_on:
  - discovery--triage-router
  parallel_group: domain-managers
  agent_type: general
  description: "You are the Jurisdiction-Challenge Domain Manager (a manager). Read /workspace/routing.md and take ONLY the opinions routed to jurisdiction-challenge (standing, jurisdiction, timeliness, Blue & Gold waiver, 28 U.S.C. 1491(b) as the dispositive issue). For EACH opinion in your bucket, spawn one leaf worker via the task tool with subagent_type \"explore\". Give the worker the protest_id, opinion_url, and text_url and these exact instructions - \"Ensure the opinion is on disk (`curl -sL <text_url> -o /workspace/records/<protest_id>.txt` if missing), READ /workspace/records/<protest_id>.txt in full, then WRITE a JSON file /workspace/records/<protest_id>.json containing exactly these keys - protest_id, case_name (the exact caption), docket_number, date_filed (YYYY-MM-DD, reissued/public date), authoring_judge (the judge who signed it), opinion_url, protest_ground (jurisdiction-challenge), outcome (granted/denied/dismissed/other based on the court's actual ruling), relief_sought, word_count (integer whitespace-split count of the opinion text), dispositive_quote (ONE sentence copied VERBATIM from the opinion stating the ruling -- copy it exactly, do not paraphrase). Use the write tool for this single small JSON file, then read it back to confirm valid JSON and that the dispositive_quote text actually appears in the opinion file. Do not invent fields for an opinion you could not read.\" After your workers finish, run `ls /workspace/records/*.json` and re-dispatch a worker for any opinion in your bucket missing a JSON file. Report how many JSON records your bucket produced."

the cohort must include at least 5 distinct authoring judges and cover at least 3 of the 4 protest grounds); (2) for each record, OPEN the matching /workspace/records/<protest_id>.txt and confirm the dispositive_quote text appears in it -- if not, replace the quote with a sentence from that text that contains a ruling verb (ORDERED, GRANTED, DENIED, DISMISSED) so the quote is genuinely verbatim; (3) computes the judge-level cross-tab, the ground-level cross-tab (success_rate = granted/case_count to 2 dp), and the monthly filing counts in the script; (4) uses openpyxl to write /logs/agent/output.xlsx with exactly six worksheets using the EXACT sheet names and column headers from the task instruction - protest_ledger (protest_id, case_name, docket_number, date_filed, authoring_judge, opinion_url, protest_ground, outcome, relief_sought, word_count, dispositive_quote), judge_analysis (authoring_judge, opinions_authored, granted_count, denied_count, dismissed_count, other_count, most_common_ground), ground_taxonomy (protest_ground, case_count, granted, denied, dismissed, other, success_rate, example_case), frozen_citation_packet (protest_id, opinion_url, retrieved_date, accessible), briefing_memo (row 2 = a 200+ word client narrative naming specific judges and cases, the overall grant rate, the strongest and weakest grounds, and a recommendation), summary (metric/value rows: total_opinions, distinct_judges, grounds_covered, granted_count, denied_count, dismissed_count, other_count, overall_grant_rate, earliest_date_filed, latest_date_filed, all internally consistent with protest_ledger); (5) uses matplotlib to write the four charts /logs/agent/chart_outcome_split.png, /logs/agent/chart_outcomes_by_ground.png, /logs/agent/chart_judge_activity.png, /logs/agent/chart_filing_timeline.png. Run the script, then run `ls -la /logs/agent/` to confirm output.xlsx and all four PNGs exist and are non-empty. If some records are missing fields, fill the workbook with whatever valid rows exist rather than aborting. Do not finish until /logs/agent/output.xlsx and the four charts exist."

version: '1.0'
coordination_pattern: hierarchical
# depends_on records DATAFLOW: which sub-task's output this one opens. It is not a
# spawn tree. In the intended run the root dispatches discover-catalog, the eight
# instrument coordinators and the synthesis chain; each coordinator then spawns its
# own topic-block readers  -  parent chain orchestrator -> coordinator -> reader
# (dag_depth = 3). A synthesizer aggregates without necessarily spawning readers.
sub_tasks:
  - id: discover-catalog
    description: >-
      Lock the cohort. Read /input_artifacts/claim_catalog.json,
      /input_artifacts/concordance_rulebook.md and /input_artifacts/source_manifest.json. Write
      /logs/agent/catalog_lock.json listing all 140 claim_ids grouped by instrument_id with
      source_url and topic_cluster. Do not fetch instrument bodies yet.
    depends_on:
      []
    parallel_group: discovery
  - id: read-wh_commit-b1
    description: >-
      Instrument reader for WH_COMMIT, block 1. Fetch the instrument at the catalog source_url for
      WH_COMMIT. For each of [CLM-0001, CLM-0002, CLM-0003, CLM-0004, CLM-0005] locate the passage
      named by focus_prompt, write obligation_paraphrase in your own words, copy a 12-60 word
      evidence_quote that appears in the instrument, assign force_class, duty_bearer, testability,
      scope_class from the rulebook, compute binding_score, and write a grounded note. Write
      /logs/agent/rows_read-wh_commit-b1.json. Answer to coord-wh_commit. Do not invent quotes.
    depends_on:
      - discover-catalog
    parallel_group: readers-wh_commit

      (string topic_cluster list), most_overclaimed_pairs (objects with instrument_a,
      instrument_b, topic_cluster, why), family_comparison, recommended_actions and summary.
      Before finishing, recompute coverage, quote grounding, binding arithmetic, crosswalk
      coherence and chart agreement from the matrix itself and repair outputs in place so the
      docket, ledger, charts and briefing agree; most_overclaimed_pairs must reference real
      shared groups. Do not invent force, quotes, claims or crosswalks the matrix does not support.
    depends_on:
      - write-docket
      - build-charts
      - analyse-force
      - analyse-crosswalk
      - interim-assembly
    parallel_group: synthesis

      Recompute /logs/agent/chart_data.json directly from the final case index, three registers, and recurrence map. Preserve exact labels and values for downstream plots and the learning board.
    depends_on:
      - build-recurrence-map
      - audit-canonical-casebook
    parallel_group: executive-data
  - id: compose-playbook
    description: >-
      Write /logs/agent/fleet_learning_playbook.md from the audited registers, all case briefs, action audit, and recurrence map. Use every required heading, give all 24 cases a substantive pre-index paragraph, place a resolving event ID, barrier ID, and action ID beside the relevant case and mechanism language, explain transfer limits and competing explanations, and separate official actions from fleet proposals. Re-open the output and write /logs/agent/work/playbook_audit.json only after those conditions pass; an identifier dump is not evidence coverage.
    depends_on:
      - build-recurrence-map
      - audit-canonical-casebook
    parallel_group: executive-data
  - id: render-all-visuals
    description: >-
      Render all three required files from chart_data.json and the relevant audited artifacts: /logs/agent/barrier_pathway_heatmap.png, /logs/agent/action_lineage_gap.png, and /logs/agent/casualty_recurrence_network.png. Use a distinct subject-appropriate form for each, readable labels at native resolution, visible legends, non-trivial dimensions, and no shared placeholder image; verify all three open and have distinct hashes.
    depends_on:
      - derive-chart-data
    parallel_group: visual-rendering
  - id: build-learning-board
    description: >-
      Build /logs/agent/marine_learning_board.html from chart_data.json and the audited analytical artifacts. Embed no remote assets; display the exact chart-data SHA-256 plus every required case, barrier, action, and recurrence metric; include all 24 filter-contract case objects; and implement the two required marked pure JavaScript functions, visible controls, filtered-case rendering, and empty-state behavior. Execute the prescribed headless DOM trials and write /logs/agent/work/board_audit.json with the observed results.
    depends_on:
      - render-all-visuals
    parallel_group: board-integration
  - id: release-preflight
    description: >-
      Read all 35 required final deliverables without editing them. Recompute case and register counts, chart collections and SHA-256; resolve every case, event, barrier, action, theme, intervention, brief, and playbook reference; inspect the three PNGs; and repeat the board filter/render trials. Write a machine-readable /logs/agent/work/release_preflight.json listing each invariant as PASS or a precise path-and-field defect.
    depends_on:
      - compose-playbook
      - build-learning-board
    parallel_group: release-preflight
  - id: final-remediation-and-release
    description: >-
      Use release_preflight.json as the sole repair queue. Correct every reported final-artifact defect without inventing evidence, then rerun the full preflight until no issue remains. Do not weaken schemas or delete difficult records to obtain a pass. Write /logs/agent/work/release_complete.json with final file inventory, counts, link totals, chart-data SHA-256, image hashes, board trial results, and an empty unresolved_issues array.
    depends_on:
      - release-preflight
    parallel_group: final-release

Source file(s):
  generated_tasks_Knowledge_research\226de6b995cd43b098b2a4b25d8f4d31-SWARMBENCH-HIERARCHICAL-KNOWLEDGE-RESEARCH-CROSS-JURISDICTION-MEDICINES-DIVERGENCE-DOSSIER\decomposition.yaml
```yaml
# Decomposition for the cross-jurisdiction medicines divergence review.
#
# The cohort is 120 published assessment documents totalling roughly 29.6 million
# characters, about 7.4 million tokens. The work divides along two boundaries that
# already exist in the material: which agency published the document, because the three
# agencies write to entirely different house styles, and which therapeutic area the
# molecule sits in, because that is what makes a batch of documents coherent to read
# together. Comparison across agencies cannot begin until the per-agency reading is
# done, which is why the pairing work sits below the readers rather than beside them.
#
# depends_on records what a sub-task CONSUMES, so the edges run upwards: a coordinator
# depends on its own readers because it checks the rows they return, and an agency lead
# depends on its own coordinators. Ownership runs the other way -- orchestrator to agency
# lead to area coordinator to reader, four levels -- and is stated in the descriptions,
# where each node names who it is answerable to and who works beneath it.

version: 1
coordination_pattern: hierarchical
sub_tasks:
  - id: build-index
    parallel_group: 1
    depends_on: []
    outputs: [/logs/agent/work/index.json]
    description: >-
      Read /workspace/input_artifacts/assessment_catalog.csv, /workspace/input_artifacts/divergence_rulebook.md and /workspace/input_artifacts/source_manifest.json. Produce a working index of the 120 records grouped by agency and then by therapeutic area, recording for each record its record_id, molecule, inn, therapeutic area, agency and document URL, and recording for each of the 50 molecules which agencies assessed it and therefore which comparison pairs exist for it. Confirm the cohort is 120 records, 50 molecules and 90 pairs before anything else starts. Produce no findings: this step establishes what has to be read, not what the documents say.

  - id: read-eu-01
    parallel_group: 2
    depends_on: [build-index]
    outputs: [/logs/agent/work/rows-eu-01.json]
    description: >-
      Read the full published EMA assessment document for these 4 records and produce one completed matrix row for each: BYLVAY-EU, ELFABRIO-EU, IMCIVREE-EU, LIVMARLI-EU. The molecules are Bylvay (odevixibat), Elfabrio (pegunigalsidase alfa), Imcivree (setmelanotide), Livmarli (maralixibat chloride). You answer to coord-eu-1, the area coordinator for this block, and these record ids are yours alone. Fetch each document from the URL the catalogue gives for that record and read it; the catalogue carries identity only and contains no finding. For each record record the approved indication as a verbatim span of between 12 and 60 words, the population restriction, the basis for it, the residual uncertainty the agency treated as most consequential, a second verbatim span in which the agency names that uncertainty, the post-authorisation obligation, the identifier of the study the decision rested on, the narrowing score computed by rulebook section 7, and a one-sentence reviewer note in your own words. Both spans must be copied exactly from the document you fetched. Report an honest absent-finding value rather than a guess where the document does not support one.

version: '1.0'
coordination_pattern: hierarchical
# depends_on records DATAFLOW: which sub-task's output this one opens. It is not a
# spawn tree. In the realized run the root dispatches the discovery step, the fifteen
# family coordinators, the five domain leads and the synthesis chain directly, and
# each coordinator then spawns its own readers and its own evidence gatherers -- so
# the parent chain is orchestrator -> coordinator -> reader, which is the three levels
# task.toml reports as dag_depth. A domain lead aggregates its coordinators' output
# without dispatching them.
#
# Two partitions of the same corpus run side by side. Readers are sharded by
# REQUIREMENT and trace ancestry; evidence gatherers are sharded by DOCUMENT and
# collect the errata and registry spans, which belong to a document rather than to a
# requirement. The coordinator joins them.
orchestrator_directive: >-
  You are the root orchestrator for the normative lineage dossier. Follow this decomposition exactly: each
  sub_tasks entry maps to one task call; do not merge, skip, or invent sub-tasks. Dispatch discover-catalog
  first, then spawn the 15 family coordinators and 5 domain leads as siblings alongside the synthesis chain in
  depends_on order. Coordinators spawn their own readers AND their own evidence gatherers, and the two run side
  by side: a reader is sharded by requirement and traces ancestry, a gatherer is sharded by document and
  collects errata and registry spans. Do not let a reader do a gatherer's fetching or the errata pages will be
  fetched many times over. Leads aggregate coordinator outputs without spawning them. There are 280 requirements
  across 70 protocol families and this will not fit in one context, so do not attempt any part of the reading
  yourself. Keep all deliverables under /logs/agent/ and preserve quotations character for character when
  merging.

      files. Build the conformance and errata picture: the registry_conformance distribution across all 280 rows,
      every row whose conformance is NOT_FOUND or REFERENCE_MISMATCH listed with its registry_name, the
      errata_bearing distribution, and the rows carrying VERIFIED_AFFECTS listed out with their errata_id. These are
      the rows an implementer is most likely to get wrong, so check each one against the row's own
      registry_dependency and flag any row claiming a conformance verdict while declaring no registry dependency at
      all, any row claiming an errata verdict other than NONE without an errata_id, and any row whose
      registry_dependency is not NONE without a registry_quote. Write /logs/agent/registry_analysis.json. Do not
      read any RFC and do not rewrite the matrix.

      /logs/agent/lineage_matrix.csv. Write the per-family prose: for every protocol family in the cohort, a
      lineage_arc of 60 to 300 words saying what has happened to that family's requirements over time, which of them
      have moved most, and what an implementer working from the current documents would most need warning about.
      Name real req_ids and real RFC numbers from that family, and make every claim true to the row it rests on.
      Then assemble the finished /logs/agent/supersession_register.json, carrying each family's requirement count,
      mean stability, change_class counts, errata_bearing_counts, unstable req_ids and arc, plus the world_rules

      change_classes, strength_distribution, stability_scores and errata_bearing -- to /logs/agent/chart_data.json,
      and render the one chart the brief asks for, /logs/agent/change_classes.png. Every series must be recomputed
      from the matrix rather than copied from any earlier partial file, so that the chart and the data agree with
      the finished matrix and not with the interim one. The chart is read back by a grader that transcribes it, so
      print each bar's category label and its height where a reader can see them, and label both axes. Do not read
      any RFC.
    depends_on:
      - analyse-lineage
      - analyse-registry
      - interim-assembly
    parallel_group: writing
  - id: compose-briefing
    description: >-
      Final assembly. Consume /logs/agent/supersession_register.json, /logs/agent/chart_data.json,
      /logs/agent/lineage_analysis.json, /logs/agent/registry_analysis.json and /logs/agent/lineage_matrix.csv, and
      write /logs/agent/output.json: requirements_reviewed, change_class_counts, unstable_requirements across all
      families, strength_counts, registry_conformance_counts, stability_score_range, an errata_and_registry_findings
      section giving what the errata and registry sweep actually turned up, a family_comparison naming specific
      families and specific req_ids, three to six recommended_actions that follow from what was actually found, and
      a summary. Then reconcile all five deliverables against one another: every count in output.json must equal the
      same count in chart_data.json and in the matrix, and every req_id listed as unstable must carry a
      stability_score of 4 or below in the matrix. Where they disagree, the matrix wins and the others are corrected
      to match it. Do not read any RFC.
    depends_on:
      - write-arcs
      - build-charts
      - analyse-lineage
      - analyse-registry
      - interim-assembly
    parallel_group: synthesis

    depends_on:
      - render-visuals
      - write-board-playbook
    parallel_group: final

sub_tasks:

  - id: lead--g1

task_id: 9758278b13ad4472b45a51a0e10afd40
coordination_pattern: hierarchical
strategy: Twenty-five two-paper reader managers author disjoint final notes plus machine-readable row and edge packets, six family-lead managers repair every note and packet against the exact roster question and source text, and a final integration manager concatenates the audited packets without rewriting evidence.
manager_protocol:
  prompt_propagation: >-
    The orchestrator must paste the exact_target_rule, direct_authorship_rule,
    reader_handoff, row_packet_rule, and edge_packet_rule into every
    reader-manager prompt; paste family_handoff into every family-manager
    prompt; and paste integration_preflight plus every preflight checklist
    into the integration-manager prompt. Referring vaguely to this protocol is
    insufficient.
  leaf_delegation: >-
    Every manager created for a sub_tasks entry must spawn exactly one explore
    leaf worker and wait for that worker. Reader and family managers delegate
    their assigned source work to that leaf, then independently inspect and
    repair the returned files. The integration manager must not spawn
    note-writing helpers because all notes already exist; it may spawn exactly
    one explore leaf for a complete preflight, wait for it, and repair every
    reported failure.
  artifact_rule: >-
    A manager may return only after its declared output_path exists, has been
    read back, and contains the requested substantive artifact. A progress
    summary, proposed method, or unexecuted checklist is not a handoff.
  exact_target_rule: >-
    Before searching a paper, every reader manager and leaf must open
    /input_artifacts/measurement_roster.md and copy the exact note ID, paper ID,
    Question, source unit, dimension, and canonical unit for each assigned paper
    into its working packet. The exact Question controls the target. Adjacent
    numbers, another amplitude bin, a non-detection mentioned elsewhere, or a
    related sample size are distractors and must never replace the answer to
    that Question.
  direct_authorship_rule: >-
    Each reader pair owns two unique final paths under
    /logs/agent/output/observation_notes/ and must write the complete notes
    there before returning. It must also write one independent
    header-plus-one-row packet at /workspace/row_packets/<note_id>.csv for each
    note, and /workspace/edge_sections/<pair_id>.md with one complete atlas
    section. Per-note packets prevent two concurrent family leads from
    overwriting each other. No later manager may regenerate a note or evidence
    quote from memory.
  reader_handoff: >-
    Each reader manager must preserve two complete final notes, two independent
    one-row register packets, and one edge section for its assigned pair. For
    every note, first locate the answer to the exact roster Question, then copy
    exactly one 8-to-100-word contiguous source substring that contains that
    answer as printed. A block quote line begins with > followed by that
    substring only; never add enclosing quotation marks, alter punctuation,
    convert a spelled number such as Five to 5, or paraphrase inside the quote.
    The note must name the exact note and paper IDs, contain exactly the five
    required level-two headings, contain 280 to 650 words after quote removal,
    state raw and canonical values in explanatory sentences, and end with a
    source-specific boundary sentence containing cannot infer, context-limited,
    uncertain, calibration, inferred, or model language plus at least two terms
    from the roster Question/unit/dimension.
  row_packet_rule: >-
    Each per-note packet uses the exact ten-column register header plus exactly
    one data row. Its raw and canonical values must answer the roster Question,
    not a nearby quantity. The normalization_basis must contain the note ID, the
    measurement dimension, the canonical unit, and the applicable
    unit_rules.json phrase. The uncertainty section must justify the chosen
    status with source-specific evidence and explicitly use the matching cue:
    observed or measured for DIRECT_OBSERVATION, model-derived or inferred for
    MODEL_DERIVED, calibration for CALIBRATION_DEPENDENT, and context-limited or
    uncertain for CONTEXT_LIMITED.
  edge_packet_rule: >-
    Each edge file begins with exactly ### <pair_id>, supplies at least 60 words,
    uses title/question terminology from both papers, and contains this
    information in one sentence: the larger note ID, larger paper ID, the word
    larger/greater/higher, and the exact oracle-independent ratio or absolute
    gap computed from the two audited canonical values. Preferred grammar is
    The larger record is note X (paper Y); it is larger than note A (paper B),
    with an exact absolute difference of G UNIT. Both identities and the numeric
    relation must remain in that same sentence.
  family_handoff: >-
    Each family manager must audit and directly repair every assigned final note
    and its row packet. For each note it must re-open the exact roster entry and
    paper, prove that the block quote after removing only the leading > is an
    exact source substring containing the roster answer, verify all five
    headings and 280-to-650-word prose, check both values and unit arithmetic,
    enforce a verifier-recognizable boundary sentence, and replace enum-only
    status prose with a source-specific explanation. A ledger that merely lists
    failures without repairing the corresponding final note and row packet is
    incomplete.
  integration_preflight: >-
    The integration manager must concatenate the already audited row packets in
    s01-to-s50 order and the already audited edge sections in pair_roster order.
    It must never rewrite, summarize, spell-normalize, or replace final note
    quotes/values. Its one explore leaf must audit all 50 notes, all 50 rows, and
    all 25 sections rather than sampling. The leaf must print a per-ID PASS/FAIL
    table for heading order, exact quote substring and raw answer, IDs,
    raw/canonical explanatory sentences, 280-to-650-word prose, boundary cue
    plus roster terms, row arithmetic/status cue, and single-sentence edge
    direction. The manager repairs every FAIL, reruns the full audit until all
    IDs pass, and rereads final deliverables before returning.
  note_preflight:
  - Exactly s01.md through s50.md exist under /logs/agent/output/observation_notes/, with no missing or extra Markdown files.
  - Every note names its note ID and paper ID, contains all five required level-two headings, contains 280 to 650 words excluding its single block quote, and uses source-specific prose rather than a repeated template.
  - Every quote, after removing only the Markdown > prefix, is an exact contiguous substring of that note's matching paper, is 8 to 100 words, contains the answer to the exact roster Question as printed, has no added enclosing quote marks, and is not copied from another paper or a references section.
  - Every evidence-and-normalization section states the requested raw value and canonical value in explanatory sentences, and every comparability-boundary section gives a source-specific limitation.
  register_preflight:
  - /logs/agent/output/measurement_provenance_register.csv has the exact ten-column header and exactly 50 roster-ordered data rows.
  - Every row preserves the exact note ID, paper ID, family, dimension, units, raw value, canonical value, audit-keyed normalization basis, and note-supported controlled interpretation status.
  atlas_preflight:
  - /logs/agent/output/cross_scale_comparability_atlas.md begins with the required portfolio scope paragraph and contains exactly one level-three heading for each of the 25 roster edge IDs.
  - Every edge section contains one explicit relation sentence that jointly names both note IDs and both paper IDs, names the larger paper and note, uses larger, greater, or higher language, and states the exact ratio or absolute difference in that same sentence.
  - Every edge explains a source-specific comparability boundary, and the full atlas covers all six research families without claiming scientific equivalence from matching dimensions alone.

      several distinct grounded findings across more than one canonical topic to
      /logs/agent/findings_bdu.csv in the schema finding_id,unit_code,unit_name,county,topic,
      priority_finding,quote,source_url -- verbatim quote plus own-words finding that differs
      from the quote, county from this unit's counties in instruction.md, source_url the
      official link. Record a gap rather than guessing if the plan will not open.

    description: >-

      risk; cross-cutting themes; an outliers and where-to-concentrate-effort section; an

      recommendations; and an appendix listing every unit and its plan link. Say something

      units, counties and counts.

```yaml
version: "1.0"
coordination_pattern: "hierarchical"

# GENUINE NESTED HIERARCHY realised in the spawn tree:
#   orchestrator
#     -> discover-catalog            (1 leaf discovery agent)
#     -> stage-01 .. stage-06        (6 fetch-pool leaves, spawned DIRECTLY by the orchestrator)
#     -> batch-01 .. batch-24        (24 batch managers, spawned DIRECTLY by the orchestrator)
#          each manager -> one reader worker per assigned system (~3 leaf workers)
#     -> synthesize                  (1 reducer)
# The orchestrator itself spawns 32 sub-agents directly (discover + 6 fetch-pool + 24 managers +
# synthesize), and each manager spawns its own leaf reader workers, so the realised parent chain
# orchestrator -> batch manager -> reader worker is three levels deep.
#
# WHY A SEPARATE FETCH TIER: the plan PDFs live on a public Internet Archive host that rate-limits
# and drops connections once more than a handful of requests hit it at once. So a small fetch pool of
# 6 agents (stage-01..06) fetches the ~70 plans a few at a time, with backoff, and stages each plan's
# extracted text at /workspace/plan_texts/<system_id>.txt. The 24 batch managers and their ~70 reader
# workers then read that already-staged local text (no network), so the heavy semantic reading and
# classification -- the load-bearing judgment work -- is parallelised freely without ever hammering
# the source. The 6 fetch-pool agents partition the 70 plans (~12 each); the 24 managers partition the
# same cohort into disjoint id batches; every reader worker reads exactly one plan's staged text. The
# widest tier is the ~70 reader workers. Descriptions state WHAT each node owns (its scope, its output
# path, its parent) -- the substantive task spec (how to classify, quote, and note) lives in the
# shared instruction.md and readiness_rulebook.md that every agent reads.
sub_tasks:

  # -------- Level 2 (orchestrator-spawned): discovery --------
  - id: discover-catalog
    parallel_group: discover
    subagent_type: explore
    depends_on: []
    description: >-
      Read /input_artifacts/system_catalog.json, /input_artifacts/readiness_rulebook.md and
      /input_artifacts/source_manifest.json, and write /workspace/catalog_index.json listing every
      system_id in catalog order with its plan_url and county. Do not spawn sub-agents, and do not
      fetch, classify, or score anything.

  # host rate-limits under concurrent load), staging each plan's text for the reader tier to read.

    parallel_group: stage

    description: >-
